# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

```plantuml
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary: Counts the total number of print-format book items in this order,
only if the order status is completed.

Algorithm:
  if this.status != COMPLETED: return 0
  count = 0
  for each itemLine in this.itemLines:
      if itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
          count = count + itemLine.quantity
  return count

Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, BookItem, BookItemType, OrderStatus
Input: None
Output: int - total count of print-format book items; 0 if order not completed or none found
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns total quantity of print format book items in completed order; 0 otherwise

Order::handleOrder
Summary: Processes an existing order by setting its status to COMPLETED and
updating library item statuses from HOLD to LOAN for print format books or disc digital items.

Algorithm:
  this.status = COMPLETED
  for each itemLine in this.itemLines:
      if itemLine.libraryItem.status == HOLD:
          if (itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT) OR
             (itemLine.libraryItem is DigitalItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.option == DISC):
              itemLine.libraryItem.status = LOAN

Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, BookItem, BookItemType, DigitalItem, DigitalItemOption
Input: None
Output: void
Pre-condition: Order must exist and be in PENDING status (implied by requirement)
Post-condition: Order status is COMPLETED; eligible library items (print/disc) with HOLD status become LOAN; other items retain their status

Order::addItemLine
Summary: Adds a new item line to the order if the order is pending, the library item is available,
and no duplicate item line (same library item) exists. Modifies library item status to HOLD
only if the item is a print format book or disc digital item.

Algorithm:
  if this.status != PENDING: return false
  if itemLine.libraryItem.status != AVAILABLE: return false
  for each existingLine in this.itemLines:
      if existingLine.libraryItem == itemLine.libraryItem: return false
  this.itemLines.add(itemLine)
  if (itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT) OR
     (itemLine.libraryItem is DigitalItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.option == DISC):
      itemLine.libraryItem.status = HOLD
  return true

Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, BookItem, BookItemType, DigitalItem, DigitalItemOption
Input: itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to add
Output: boolean - true if added, false if validation fails
Pre-condition: itemLine must not be null; itemLine.libraryItem must not be null
Post-condition: If successful, itemLine is added and library item status may be set to HOLD; returns true. Otherwise returns false.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary: Removes an item line from an existing order in Pending status.
Resets the library item's status to AVAILABLE if it was previously HOLD (for print/disc items).

Algorithm:
  if this.status != PENDING: return -1
  if this.itemLines.remove(itemLine) == true:
      if itemLine.libraryItem.status == HOLD:
          if (itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT) OR
             (itemLine.libraryItem is DigitalItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.option == DISC):
              itemLine.libraryItem.status = AVAILABLE
      return this.itemLines.size()
  return -1

Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, BookItem, BookItemType, DigitalItem, DigitalItemOption
Input: itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to remove
Output: int - updated count of item lines if successful; -1 if order is completed or item not found
Pre-condition: itemLine must not be null
Post-condition: If successful, itemLine is removed, library item status may be reset to AVAILABLE; returns updated count. Otherwise returns -1.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary: Calculates the total number of downloads for this specific Audio digital item
by counting completed orders where this item appears as a downloadable item.

Algorithm:
  total = 0
  for each order in orders:
      if order.status == COMPLETED:
          for each itemLine in order.itemLines:
              if itemLine.libraryItem == this AND this.option == DOWNLOADABLE AND this.type == AUDIO:
                  total = total + itemLine.quantity
  return total

Related Features: status (inherited from LibraryItem), option, type
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, OrderStatus, DigitalItemOption, DigitalItemType
Input: orders : List<Order> - list of orders to search through
Output: int - total download count; 0 if no downloads found
Pre-condition: orders must not be null
Post-condition: returns total quantity of this digital item in completed orders where option is DOWNLOADABLE and type is AUDIO

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information

  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Adds a new item line to the order if the order is pending, the library item is available,
   * and no duplicate item line (same library item) exists. Modifies library item status to HOLD
   * only if the item is a print format book or disc digital item.
   * 
   * Algorithm:
   *   if this.status != PENDING: return false
   *   if itemLine.libraryItem.status != AVAILABLE: return false
   *   for each existingLine in this.itemLines:
   *       if existingLine.libraryItem == itemLine.libraryItem: return false
   *   this.itemLines.add(itemLine)
   *   if (itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT) OR
   *      (itemLine.libraryItem is DigitalItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.option == DISC):
   *       itemLine.libraryItem.status = HOLD
   *   return true
   * 
   * Related Features: status, itemLines
   * Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, BookItem, BookItemType, DigitalItem, DigitalItemOption
   * Input: itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to add
   * Output: boolean - true if added, false if validation fails
   * Pre-condition: itemLine must not be null; itemLine.libraryItem must not be null
   * Post-condition: If successful, itemLine is added and library item status may be set to HOLD; returns true. Otherwise returns false.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model itemLineRequired="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING) {
      return false;
    }
    if (itemLine.getLibraryItem().getStatus() != LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE) {
      return false;
    }
    for (ItemLine existingLine : getItemLines()) {
      if (existingLine.getLibraryItem() == itemLine.getLibraryItem()) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    getItemLines().add(itemLine);
    LibraryItem libItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
    if (libItem instanceof BookItem) {
      if (((BookItem) libItem).getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT) {
        libItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
      }
    } else if (libItem instanceof DigitalItem) {
      if (((DigitalItem) libItem).getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC) {
        libItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Removes an item line from an existing order in Pending status.
   * Resets the library item's status to AVAILABLE if it was previously HOLD (for print/disc items).
   * Returns updated count of item lines if successful, -1 if order is completed.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if this.status != PENDING: return -1
   *   if this.itemLines.remove(itemLine) == true:
   *       if itemLine.libraryItem.status == HOLD:
   *           if (itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT) OR
   *              (itemLine.libraryItem is DigitalItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.option == DISC):
   *               itemLine.libraryItem.status = AVAILABLE
   *       return this.itemLines.size()
   *   return this.itemLines.size()  // item not found, return current count (or 0 if empty)
   * Related Features: status, itemLines
   * Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, BookItem, BookItemType, DigitalItem, DigitalItemOption
   * Input: itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to remove
   * Output: int - updated count of item lines if successful; -1 if order is completed
   * Pre-condition: itemLine must not be null
   * Post-condition: If order is pending and itemLine is found, itemLine is removed, library item status may be reset to AVAILABLE; returns updated count. If order is completed, returns -1.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model itemLineRequired="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int removeItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING) {
      return -1;
    }
    if (getItemLines().remove(itemLine)) {
      LibraryItem libItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
      if (libItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD) {
        if (libItem instanceof BookItem && ((BookItem) libItem).getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT) {
          libItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
        } else if (libItem instanceof DigitalItem && ((DigitalItem) libItem).getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC) {
          libItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
        }
      }
      return getItemLines().size();
    }
    return getItemLines().size();
```java
  }
```

   *   total = 0

  public int calculateTotalDownloads(final EList<Order> orders)
  {
    int total = 0;
    for (Order order : orders) {

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

```plantuml
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary: Counts the total number of print-format book items in this order, 
but only if the order status is completed. Returns 0 if order is not completed or no print books exist.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != COMPLETED: return 0
  2. Initialize count = 0
  3. For each itemLine in this.itemLines:
     Let libItem = itemLine.libraryItem
     If libItem is BookItem AND libItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
        count += itemLine.quantity
  4. Return count
Related Features: this.status, this.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, itemLine.quantity, 
                  libItem.type (for BookItem)
Related Classes: BookItem, BookItemType, OrderStatus, ItemLine, LibraryItem
Input: None (operates on the current Order instance)
Output: int - the total quantity of print-format book items in the order, or 0 if order is not completed or none found
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: The returned value is the sum of quantities of item lines referencing Print Format BookItems 
                if the order is completed; otherwise 0. No state changes occur.

Order::addItemLine
Summary: Adds a new item line to the order if validation passes. Validates that order is pending, 
library item is available, and no duplicate item line exists. For print-format book items or disc 
digital items, changes the library item status to HOLD.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != PENDING: return false
  2. Let libItem = itemLine.libraryItem
  3. If libItem.status != AVAILABLE: return false
  4. For each existingLine in this.itemLines:
     If existingLine.libraryItem == libItem: return false
  5. Determine if status change needed:
     If libItem is BookItem AND libItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT: set libItem.status = HOLD
     Else if libItem is DigitalItem AND libItem.option == DISC: set libItem.status = HOLD
     Else: keep libItem.status = AVAILABLE
  6. Add itemLine to this.itemLines
  7. Return true
Related Features: this.status, this.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, itemLine.quantity, 
                  libItem.status, libItem.type (for BookItem), libItem.option (for DigitalItem)
Related Classes: LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, ItemLine
Input: itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to add, which must have a valid libraryItem reference and quantity > 0
Output: boolean - true if item line was successfully added, false if validation fails
Pre-condition: itemLine is not null, itemLine.libraryItem is not null, itemLine.quantity > 0
Post-condition: If true returned: itemLine is added to this.itemLines, and library item status may be updated to HOLD.
                If false returned: no changes are made to the order or library items.

Order::handleOrder
Summary: Processes an existing order by setting its status to completed, and for print-format book items 
or disc digital items that are currently on hold, changes their status to loan.
Algorithm:
  1. Set this.status = COMPLETED
  2. For each itemLine in this.itemLines:
     Let libItem = itemLine.libraryItem
     If libItem.status == HOLD:
        If libItem is BookItem AND libItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT: set libItem.status = LOAN
        Else if libItem is DigitalItem AND libItem.option == DISC: set libItem.status = LOAN
  3. Return (void)
Related Features: this.status, this.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, libItem.status, 
                  libItem.type (for BookItem), libItem.option (for DigitalItem)
Related Classes: BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, ItemLine
Input: None (operates on the current Order instance)
Output: void - no return value
Pre-condition: The order exists and has at least one item line (implicitly required for meaningful operation)
Post-condition: Order status is set to COMPLETED. All print-format book items and disc digital items 
                that were on HOLD are now on LOAN. Other items retain their previous status.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary: Removes an item line from an existing order that is in Pending status. 
If the library item was a print-format book or disc digital item that was on HOLD, 
resets its status to AVAILABLE. Returns the updated count of item lines, or -1 if order is not pending.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != PENDING: return -1
  2. Let libItem = itemLine.libraryItem
  3. Remove itemLine from this.itemLines
  4. Determine if status reset needed:
     If libItem.status == HOLD:
        If libItem is BookItem AND libItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT: set libItem.status = AVAILABLE
        Else if libItem is DigitalItem AND libItem.option == DISC: set libItem.status = AVAILABLE
        Else: keep libItem.status = HOLD (should not occur based on requirements, but safe)
     Else: keep libItem.status unchanged
  5. Return this.itemLines.size() (the updated count)
Related Features: this.status, this.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, libItem.status, 
                  libItem.type (for BookItem), libItem.option (for DigitalItem)
Related Classes: BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, ItemLine
Input: itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to remove (must be a reference to an existing item line in this order)
Output: int - the updated number of item lines in the order after removal, or -1 if order is not pending
Pre-condition: itemLine is not null and exists within this.itemLines (caller must ensure this)
Post-condition: If order is pending: itemLine is removed, library item status may be reset to AVAILABLE 
                if it was a print/disc item on HOLD. Returns new count of item lines.
                If order is not pending: no changes made, returns -1.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary: Calculates the total download count for this specific Audio digital item, 
considering only completed orders where the digital item's option is Downloadable.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize total = 0
  2. For each order in the input orders list:
     a. If order.status == COMPLETED:
        i. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
           - If itemLine.libraryItem == this (the current DigitalItem) AND this.option == DOWNLOADABLE AND this.type == AUDIO:
             total += itemLine.quantity
  3. Return total
Related Features: this.option, this.type, order.status, order.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, itemLine.quantity
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, OrderStatus, DigitalItemOption, DigitalItemType
Input: orders : List<Order> - a list of orders to examine for download counts
Output: int - the total number of downloads (sum of quantities from matching item lines). Returns 0 if no downloads found.
Pre-condition: None (the method can be called on any DigitalItem instance with any list of orders)
Post-condition: The returned value is the sum of quantities of item lines that reference this digital item, 
                where the order is completed, the item is an Audio digital item with Downloadable option.

Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
```text
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary: Calculates the total download count for this specific Audio digital item, 
considering only completed orders where the digital item's option is Downloadable.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize total = 0
  2. For each order in the input orders list:
     a. If order.status == COMPLETED:
        i. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
           - If itemLine.libraryItem == this (the current DigitalItem) AND this.option == DOWNLOADABLE AND this.type == AUDIO:
             total += itemLine.quantity
  3. Return total
Related Features: this.option, this.type, order.status, order.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, itemLine.quantity
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, OrderStatus, DigitalItemOption, DigitalItemType
Input: orders : List<Order> - a list of orders to examine for download counts
Output: int - the total number of downloads (sum of quantities from matching item lines). Returns 0 if no downloads found.
Pre-condition: None (the method can be called on any DigitalItem instance with any list of orders)
Post-condition: The returned value is the sum of quantities of item lines that reference this digital item, 
                where the order is completed, the item is an Audio digital item with Downloadable option.

```java
/**
 * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
 * <!-- end-user-doc -->
 * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
 * Summary: Adds a new item line to the order if validation passes. Validates that order is pending, 
 * library item is available, and no duplicate item line exists. For print-format book items or disc 
 * digital items, changes the library item status to HOLD.
 * Algorithm:
 *   1. If this.status != PENDING: return false
 *   2. Let libItem = itemLine.libraryItem
 *   3. If libItem.status != AVAILABLE: return false
 *   4. For each existingLine in this.itemLines:
 *      If existingLine.libraryItem == libItem: return false
 *   5. Determine if status change needed:
 *      If libItem is BookItem AND libItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT: set libItem.status = HOLD
 *      Else if libItem is DigitalItem AND libItem.option == DISC: set libItem.status = HOLD
 *      Else: keep libItem.status = AVAILABLE
 *   6. Add itemLine to this.itemLines
 *   7. Return true
 * Related Features: this.status, this.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, itemLine.quantity, 
 *                   libItem.status, libItem.type (for BookItem), libItem.option (for DigitalItem)
 * Related Classes: LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, ItemLine
 * Input: itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to add, which must have a valid libraryItem reference and quantity > 0
 * Output: boolean - true if item line was successfully added, false if validation fails
 * Pre-condition: itemLine is not null, itemLine.libraryItem is not null, itemLine.quantity > 0
 * Post-condition: If true returned: itemLine is added to this.itemLines, and library item status may be updated to HOLD.
 *                 If false returned: no changes are made to the order or library items.
 * <!-- end-model-doc -->
 * @model itemLineRequired="true"
 *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
```
 * @generated NOT
 */
public boolean addItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
{
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING) {
        return false;
    }
    
    LibraryItem libItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
    
    if (libItem.getStatus() != LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE) {
        return false;
    }
    
    for (ItemLine existingLine : getItemLines()) {
        if (existingLine.getLibraryItem() == libItem) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    if (libItem instanceof BookItem) {
        BookItem bookItem = (BookItem) libItem;
        if (bookItem.getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT) {
            bookItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
        }
    } else if (libItem instanceof DigitalItem) {
        DigitalItem digitalItem = (DigitalItem) libItem;
        if (digitalItem.getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC) {
            digitalItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
        }
    }
    
    getItemLines().add(itemLine);
    return true;
}

 * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
 * <!-- end-user-doc -->

 * Summary: Counts the total number of print-format book items in this order, 
 * but only if the order status is completed. Returns 0 if order is not completed or no print books exist.

 *   2. Initialize count = 0
 *   3. For each itemLine in this.itemLines:

                count++;
            }
        }
    }
    return count;

/**
 * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
 * <!-- end-user-doc -->
 * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
 * Summary: Removes an item line from an existing order that is in Pending status. 
 * If the library item was a print-format book or disc digital item that was on HOLD, 
 * resets its status to AVAILABLE. Returns the updated count of item lines, or -1 if order is not pending.
 * Algorithm:
 *   1. If this.status != PENDING: return -1
 *   2. Let libItem = itemLine.libraryItem
 *   3. Remove itemLine from this.itemLines
 *   4. Determine if status reset needed:
 *      If libItem is BookItem AND libItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT AND libItem.status == HOLD:
 *         set libItem.status = AVAILABLE
 *      Else if libItem is DigitalItem AND libItem.option == DISC AND libItem.status == HOLD:
 *         set libItem.status = AVAILABLE
 *      Else: keep libItem.status unchanged
 *   5. Return this.itemLines.size() (the updated count)
 * Related Features: this.status, this.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, libItem.status, 
 *                   libItem.type (for BookItem), libItem.option (for DigitalItem)
 * Related Classes: BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus, ItemLine
 * Input: itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to remove (must be a reference to an existing item line in this order)
 * Output: int - the updated number of item lines in the order after removal, or -1 if order is not pending
 * Pre-condition: itemLine is not null and exists within this.itemLines (caller must ensure this)
 * Post-condition: If order is pending: itemLine is removed, library item status may be reset to AVAILABLE

 *                 If order is not pending: no changes made, returns -1.

 *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
 * @generated NOT
 */
public int removeItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
{
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING) {
        return -1;
    }
    
    LibraryItem libItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
    
    getItemLines().remove(itemLine);
    
    if (libItem instanceof BookItem) {
        BookItem bookItem = (BookItem) libItem;
        if (bookItem.getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT && bookItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD) {
            bookItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
        }
    } else if (libItem instanceof DigitalItem) {
        DigitalItem digitalItem = (DigitalItem) libItem;
        if (digitalItem.getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC && digitalItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD) {
            digitalItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
        }
    }
    
    return getItemLines().size();
}

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Calculates the total download count for this specific Audio digital item,
   * considering only completed orders where the digital item's option is Downloadable.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Initialize total = 0
   *   2. For each order in the input orders list:
   *      a. If order.status == COMPLETED:
   *         i. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   *            - If itemLine.libraryItem == this (the current DigitalItem) AND this.option == DOWNLOADABLE AND this.type == AUDIO:
   *              total += itemLine.quantity
   *   3. Return total
   * Related Features: this.option, this.type, order.status, order.itemLines, itemLine.libraryItem, itemLine.quantity
   * Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, OrderStatus, DigitalItemOption, DigitalItemType
   * Input: orders : List<Order> - a list of orders to examine for download counts
   * Output: int - the total number of downloads (sum of quantities from matching item lines). Returns 0 if no downloads found.
   * Pre-condition: None
   * Post-condition: The returned value is the sum of quantities of item lines that reference this digital item,
   *                 where the order is completed, the item is an Audio digital item with Downloadable option.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model ordersMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int calculateTotalDownloads(final EList<Order> orders)
  {
    int total = 0;
    for (Order order : orders)
    {
      if (order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.COMPLETED)
      {
        for (ItemLine itemLine : order.getItemLines())
        {
          if (itemLine.getLibraryItem() == this && getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DOWNLOADABLE && getType() == DigitalItemType.AUDIO)
          {
            total += itemLine.getQuantity();
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return total;
  }
